- **triage-officer**: 分类与范围收敛。负责把模糊输入转化为可验证的 issue 规格。 ([laozhong86/gxpm](https://github.com/laozhong86/gxpm/blob/HEAD/agents/triage-officer.md) · ★1)
- **triage-oncall**: On-call triage engineer. Groups production errors, finds the root cause in the workspace, and proposes a minimal fix with a regression test. Asks before editing. ([kartikag01/error-triage-plugin](https://github.com/kartikag01/error-triage-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/triage-oncall.agent.md))
- **triage-orchestrator**: Autonomous Jira issue triage - bulk categorization, label/component/priority suggestions, dedupe detection, linking issues to PRs and Confluence docs, batch transitions, and structured epic/sub-task creation. Triggers: 'triage incoming bugs', 'categorize unprocessed issues', 'find duplicates in this project', 'create epic with sub-tasks', 'batch-assign these issues'. ([Acendas/atlassian-suite](https://github.com/Acendas/atlassian-suite/blob/HEAD/agents/triage-orchestrator.md))
- **triage-orchestrator**: Outer-loop triage orchestrator. Consolidates the discovery engine (discover-work.sh / loop-triage, T-017), the Operating-Loop telemetry roll-up (rollup-runs.sh, T-020), and cross-run failure clusters (cluster-failures.sh, T-044) into ONE de-duplicated, BUDGET-capped triage proposal file for a human to act on. Propose-only and manual-trigger — never edits the board. Use at the start of a cycle to decide what to pick up next. ([RipsawJP/shell-team](https://github.com/RipsawJP/shell-team/blob/HEAD/agents/triage-orchestrator.md))
- **triage-orchestrator**: 設定した条件に合う Coverity の警告を、複数のエージェントで並列に処置し、同じ原因の CID を統合して、すべての結果を別のエージェントに検証させる。`copilot --agent` で起動したメインの会話として使う。 ([xclang-brain/plugin-market-place](https://github.com/xclang-brain/plugin-market-place/blob/HEAD/plugins/coverity-triage-kit/com.github.copilot/agents/triage-orchestrator.agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **triage-product**: Read-only triage perspective for incoming tasks. Challenges the user intent — who actually uses this, what problem it solves, what's in MVP, what edge cases break it, what success looks like. Invoked by /triage in parallel with triage-architect and triage-engineer. Returns a structured brief — never modifies files. ([natanrotta/claude-setup](https://github.com/natanrotta/claude-setup/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/triage-product.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **triage-project**: The `[Triage] Project sync` workflow mirrors [triage state labels](triage-labels.md) into an organization Project's `Status` field. Labels are authoritative; dragging a card does not change them. ([homarr-labs/homarr](https://github.com/homarr-labs/homarr/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/triage-project.md) · ★5.4k)
- **triage-proposer**: Reads ONE sanitised triage artifact and returns a single JSON proposal: labels from closed lists, a two-sentence summary, the quoted error and a section hint. Holds no tools but Read. Spawned by /triage, once per issue — never invoked directly for feature work. ([Zalaras/muster](https://github.com/Zalaras/muster/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/triage-proposer.md) · ★3)
- **triage-protocol**: The judgment layer between "an issue exists" and "the human approves it". Triage reads every untriaged open issue (no `fleet:` / `human:` label), judges it against the target repo's **objectives-path** and the filing rules, and produces a verdict the human acts on in one read. Two modes share the same inputs and judgment: ([jakildev/IrredenEngine](https://github.com/jakildev/IrredenEngine/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/triage-protocol.md) · ★10)
- **triage-qa**: Cross-boundary QA for alert-triage-agent — compares contracts across module seams (journal events vs eval assertions vs Pydantic models vs prompts), runs make check and make eval, executes the agent end-to-end. Use after each implemented module (incremental QA), not once at the end. ([baodq97/alert-triage-agent](https://github.com/baodq97/alert-triage-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/triage-qa.md))
- **triage-quality**: You are a fast triage reviewer. Scan PR #&lt;PR_NUMBER&gt; in &lt;REPO_SLUG&gt; for Critical and Important issues only. Skip observations, style, and conventions. ([oleg-koval/agent-skills](https://github.com/oleg-koval/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/olko-github-pr/skills/lekker-review/references/agents/triage-quality.md) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **triage-rank**: Orders candidate groups by a fixed key built from evidence. Shows the key values beside each rank. Produces no numeric score. ([portarj482-lang/Cantina](https://github.com/portarj482-lang/Cantina/blob/HEAD/global/agents/triage-rank.md))
- **triage-ranker**: Severity ranker for one confirmed triage finding. Spawn with a repo path, deployment context, and finding fields — not for general delegation. ([air-gapped/skills](https://github.com/air-gapped/skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/triage-ranker.md) · ★6)
- **triage-reader**: Reads untrusted text (an email, a web page, an issue, a ticket, a chat message, a vendor reply) and returns a small JSON summary of what it says. Has no secrets, no network and no write access; only Read. Use whenever outside text must be understood before a privileged step, so that instructions hidden in that text cannot steer the engineer. ([jcallahanp/Autonomy-Research](https://github.com/jcallahanp/Autonomy-Research/blob/HEAD/engineer/plugin/agents/triage-reader.md))
- **triage-report**: Per-finding disposition judge for one scan/audit report. Dispatched by /super-bootstrap:triage-report with a single .review/ report path. Per finding: promote (draft claim block for /super-bootstrap:log) / patch (exact doc-mechanical edit) / dup (existing card + optional new-fact Amendment) / needs-investigation (single question) / dismiss (with rationale). Returns a verdict sheet as text — writes nothing. ([RockyHong/super-bootstrap](https://github.com/RockyHong/super-bootstrap/blob/HEAD/plugins/super-bootstrap/agents/triage-report.md) · ★3)

- **triage-report-2026-07-06-pass-1**: Pass: **daily-triage, pass 1** Operator: **Codebuff agent** Predecessor: pass 0 (bootstrap) — see `triage-report-2026-07-06.md`. ([IsaacMorzy/mupla](https://github.com/IsaacMorzy/mupla/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/triage-report-2026-07-06-pass-1.md))
- **triage-report-2026-07-06-pass-12**: This report captures the output of `bin/gh-create-issues.sh`: each Pass-11 roadmap ticket mapped to one GitHub issue with `--label needs-triage`. The agent ran the burst during Pass 12 close; tickets already-open by title match were skipped (idempotent on re-run). ([IsaacMorzy/mupla](https://github.com/IsaacMorzy/mupla/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/triage-report-2026-07-06-pass-12.md))
- **triage-report-2026-07-08**: &gt; *This was generated by AI during triage.* ([IsaacMorzy/mupla](https://github.com/IsaacMorzy/mupla/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/triage-report-2026-07-08.md))
- **triage-reproduce**: You are the Reproduce station in the issue-triage assembly line. ([re-cinq/lore-assembly-lines](https://github.com/re-cinq/lore-assembly-lines/blob/HEAD/issue-triage/agents/triage-reproduce.md))
- **triage-reviewer**: SEMANTIC SECOND-OPINION on prompts where the main agent genuinely cannot disambiguate intent on a HIGH-sensitivity surface. EXPLICIT dispatch only — the agent decides when to call (no auto-trigger from a hook). Typical trigger: HIGH-sensitivity keyword in prompt + agent uncertain whether it's read-only / patch / standard / research. NOT FOR: routine classification (read prompt semantically yourself), bug debugging (use root-cause-debug skill), AC transcription (use requirement-engineer). Returns `[Semantic Review]` block with refined_hint + reason + confidence. ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/triage-reviewer.md) · ★18)
- **triage-reviewer**: Lightweight PR risk classifier. Inspects paths, size, and secret-shaped strings to decide review tier (light/standard/team). Used by /review-pr as the first step. No deep diff reading — cheapest-possible pass. ([mannepanne/useful-assets-template](https://github.com/mannepanne/useful-assets-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/triage-reviewer.md) · ★4 · 4 copies)
- **triage-reviewer** ([kenmulford/milestone-driver](https://github.com/kenmulford/milestone-driver/blob/HEAD/tests/fixtures/check-doc-toc/compliant/agents/triage-reviewer.md) · ★2 · 5 copies)
- **triage-reviewer**: Dispatched by milestone-driver's /milestone-driver:triage skill (batch or single mode) to assess whether a GitHub issue is buildable as recorded - before any code is written. Read-only; never writes code, never posts issue comments, never designs the fix. Returns a structured ISSUE / DEPENDS_ON / NEEDS_DESIGN_REVIEW / GAPS block the triage skill aggregates into an all-clear or gap table. Stack-agnostic; the profile and brief carry the stack. - **triagem-bancos-praticos**: Use proativamente quando um chamado for sobre Bancos Práticos — dúvidas, erros ou pedidos relacionados a bancos de questões/dados do Curso Prático. Aciona ao classificar, normalizar e rotear chamados desse produto. ([brendonbachie/gerenciador-de-chamados-curso-pratico](https://github.com/brendonbachie/gerenciador-de-chamados-curso-pratico/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/triagem-bancos-praticos.md))
- **triagem-cetico**: Tenta REFUTAR o veredito da triagem antes que ele seja publicado no PR do contribuidor. Chamado no passe 9. Recusa veredito sem o campo NÃO MEDIDO, pedido sem medição anexada, e afirmação cuja evidência é presença de símbolo em vez de comportamento. Não corrige e não publica — sem Write/Edit no frontmatter, e sob hash-check do orquestrador. ([melgarafael/DeskcommCRM](https://github.com/melgarafael/DeskcommCRM/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/triagem-cetico.md) · ★5.5k · 16 copies)
- **triagem-de-suporte**: Primeira resposta a uma reclamação de cliente ('piorou', 'o frame boost não liga', 'travando', print de tela): classifica o caso, diz que informação falta (log, jogo, Hz do monitor, placa, o que estava ligado), aponta o módulo provável e chama o `analista-de-log` quando houver log. Nunca diagnostica sem prova. ([Otimiza-Oficial/Otimiza](https://github.com/Otimiza-Oficial/Otimiza/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/triagem-de-suporte.md) · ★1)
- **triagem-documentos**: Porta de entrada do pacote: varre a pasta ou o ZIP do cliente, identifica cada arquivo pelo conteúdo (XML de NF-e, CT-e e NFS-e, OFX, SPED, DAS, DARF, certidões, holerites, planilhas) e gera inventário CSV com tipo, CNPJ/CPF mascarado, competência e agente indicado. Use ao receber lote de documentos desorganizado ou com nomes e extensões duvidosos. Não use para auditar o conteúdo das notas (fiscal-auditoria-xml). - **triagem-medidor**: Mede um PR de contribuidor — roda os gates da main na PRÉVIA DO MERGE e percorre o complemento do CI (o que nenhum job reprova). Chamado pela triagem no passe 3/4. ([Signaturi4/triz-for-software](https://github.com/Signaturi4/triz-for-software/blob/HEAD/agents/triz-contradiction.md))
- **triz-critic**: Solution critic for the /triz workflow. Scores ideality, may fuse ideas into a stronger one, and returns a verdict per idea. ([Signaturi4/triz-for-software](https://github.com/Signaturi4/triz-for-software/blob/HEAD/agents/triz-critic.md))
- **triz-effects**: Effects-and-information-fund specialist for the /triz workflow. ([Signaturi4/triz-for-software](https://github.com/Signaturi4/triz-for-software/blob/HEAD/agents/triz-effects.md))

- **triz-framer**: TRIZ problem framer for the /triz workflow. - **triz-ideality**: Ideality / ideal-final-result specialist for the /triz workflow. Use before any content gate. Runs in its own context — it never sees the creator's reasoning. - **truth-keeper**: Source-of-truth auditor for the Gestalt monorepo. Maps each domain to one unique SoT, finds drift across replicas, and — when the absolute SoT is unknown or ambiguous — always asks the owner before declaring truth. Use for depara kit↔Supabase (/kit-depara, depara kit supabase): diff gestalt-kit vs portfolio.kit_docs including manual /kit Save and DataGrip edits. Use when a PRD-backed feature, focused fix, or coverage gap requires behavior tracing, integration, project-native validation, and evidence, including comprehensive test authoring against acceptance criteria. Honors existing contracts and shared-workspace ownership; still requires an independent reviewer before delivery. ([wallter/trw-mcp](https://github.com/wallter/trw-mcp/blob/HEAD/src/trw_mcp/data/agents/trw-implementer.md))
- **trw-lead**: Client-neutral coordination lead. Use when work has independent streams, explicit integration boundaries, or enough risk to benefit from delegated research, implementation, testing, and review. Adapts to available harness capabilities and falls back to safe sequential coordination. Does not write production code. ([wallter/trw-mcp](https://github.com/wallter/trw-mcp/blob/HEAD/src/trw_mcp/data/agents/trw-lead.md))
- **trw-prd-groomer**: PRD authoring and grooming specialist. ([wallter/trw-mcp](https://github.com/wallter/trw-mcp/blob/HEAD/src/trw_mcp/data/agents/trw-prd-groomer.md))
- **trw-requirement-reviewer**: Read-only PRD quality review. Use when sprint readiness needs an independent, evidence-linked assessment after drafting or grooming. ([wallter/trw-mcp](https://github.com/wallter/trw-mcp/blob/HEAD/src/trw_mcp/data/agents/trw-requirement-reviewer.md))
- **trw-researcher**: Read-only investigation of code, external evidence, and competing approaches. ([wallter/trw-mcp](https://github.com/wallter/trw-mcp/blob/HEAD/src/trw_mcp/data/agents/trw-researcher.md))

- **trwm-reviewer**: Reviews a SOLVE-IT TRWM submission from the artifact alone, with no knowledge of how it was drafted. - **try-catch**: Find and fix `try`/`catch` blocks that **suppress, swallow, or hide errors** instead of handling them meaningfully. ([BenjaminBenetti/bb-claude-plugins](https://github.com/BenjaminBenetti/bb-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/bbcode/skills/autofix/agents/try-catch.md))
